     A Strategy for Growth through ESG Management


     Our Value Creation
                                                                                                                                                                              The Obayashi Group faces social challenges head on so that we can realize sustainability of “the planet, society, and people.”
                                                                                                                                                                              With ESG management as our foundation, the Group can work as a whole to increase corporate value and solve social




                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      A Strategy for Growth through ESG Management
                                                                                                                                                                              challenges through our business. Through this value creation process cycle, we will realize sustained growth while working
     ─ Value Creation Story ─                                                                                                                                                 constantly with society to create new value.

                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           OBAYASHI CORPORATE REPORT 2021
            Ms. Nishida leads the Renewable Energy Institute’s
            climate change research and initiatives. She
            conducts research and offers recommendations
            on climate and energy policy with an eye on
            Japan in 2050 and 2030. She also supports climate
            change measures by local governments and
            enterprises. Also, in her role as Vice-Chair of the
            Green Buildings Promotion Forum, she has made
            the decarbonization of the construction sector her

                                                                                                                                                                                          Physical Opportunity
                                                                                                                                                                                                                           [2018 Kinugawa River Left Bank Funadama–Isayama Section Maintenance Project]
                                    Climate-Related Information Disclosure (Disclosure Based on TCFD Recommendations)
                                                                                                                                                                                         The lower reaches of the Kinugawa River were particularly damaged by the Kanto-Tohoku Torrential Rain Disaster in
                                 https://www.obayashi.co.jp/en/sustainability/environment/tcfd.html
                                                                                                                                                                                         September 2015. Obayashi performed levee maintenance here as part of the Kinugawa Emergency Response Project, an
                                                                                                                                                                                         emergency and intensive flood control effort. During this work, we tested a drone-based surveying system that works
                                                                                                                                                                                         without reference points. Testing took advantage of the Ministry of Land, Infrastructure, Transport and Tourism’s “project for
     Risk Management                                                                                       Metrics and Targets                                                           introducing and utilizing innovative technology to dramatically improve the productivity of construction sites.” Our tests
                                                                                                                                                                                         demonstrated that the drones could perform surveying with high precision.

                                                                     Basic Policy

        At Obayashi, we understand it is the strength of each individual employee that supports our company. For that reason, we                                                                           How We Practice Human Rights Due Diligence
        establish workplaces in which diverse personnel can play an active role. When hiring, promoting, and the like, we do not
                                                                                                                                                                               Specify the main        Ascertain the actual state

                             Implement Rigorous Compliance



                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             A Foundation for ESG Management

                                                                                                                                                                                                          Workplace training on corporate ethics takes place in April and May each year for all officers and employees. The training uses a
     Ratio of employees                                       Ratio of employees taking                                                                                                                   textbook produced by the Secretariat of the Corporate Ethics Committee.      taking corporate                                         information security training
     ethics training                                    %                                                               %                                                                                 participants look at specific cases that could potentially happen in their contexts.                                                                                                                                                                                                           and ordinances such as the Antimonopoly Act of Japan, avoiding all contact with antisocial forces, and stamping out illegitimate

We will continue to give workplace training on corporate ethics to ensure that every officer and employee
                                                                                                                                                                                                          not only complies with the law but acts with a high sense of ethics and sound judgment.
                                                                                                  Basic Policy

        Obayashi’s Articles of Incorporation express our strong determination to stay aware of compliance issues, including corporate                                                                                                                          Corporate Ethics Consultation and Reporting System
        ethics. They likewise commit us to complying with laws and regulations to create a sound corporate culture. The Obayashi Code
        of Conduct moreover stipulates thorough adherence to corporate ethics throughout the Company. Top management leads the                                                                            The Obayashi Group Corporate Ethics Consultation and Reporting System is the hotline for inquiries relating to workplace
        effort for corporate ethics.                                                                                                                                                                      wrongdoing, harassment, legal violations, corruption, bribery, and other corporate ethics problems. It is available for use by
                                                                                                                                                                                                          persons concerned with the business of the Obayashi Group (regular employees, seconded employees, employees accepted on
                                                                                                                                                                                                          assignment, part-time employees, suppliers, etc.). When a report comes in, the situation is immediately investigated, the findings
                                                                       Promote the Corporate Ethics Program                                                                                               are reported, and any necessary actions are taken.
                                                                                                                                                                                                          Reporting Process through Each Contact Point
     The Corporate Ethics Program exists to help prevent corruption of all kinds. We are constantly verifying and improving the
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        Inquirer or whistleblower
     program.      and regularly review the implementation status of each measure.           PROJECT           Largest Renovation since Airport’s Opening:                                                                                   Obayashi is promoting construction DX with BIM as its work
          REPORT            Osaka International Airport Terminal Building Renovation Project in Japan                                                     platform. BIM models are now at work on many projects
                                                                                                                                                          begun in FY2021.3, and we plan to widen the scope of projects
                                                                                                                                                          where we use them. The models expand on earlier design BIM
     The passenger terminal has served as Osaka’s gateway to the sky since its completion in 1969. Obayashi carried out the large-                        models and seek to increase productivity comprehensively
     scale renovation project, which lasted more than four years, while the terminal continued to operate. The project included fire                      by integrating construction management, maintenance, and
     and seismic upgrades to bring the terminal up to current architectural standards. It also enhanced convenience by bringing                           operation.
     scattered arrival gates together. The terminal now has a walk-through commercial area leading up to the boarding gates, a                                The tentatively named Visual Process Management
     first for domestic routes, and a wood deck on the rooftop for local people to enjoy. The changes have created a smarter, more                        System, developed independently by Obayashi, uses BIM for
     sustainable airport.                                                                                                                                 construction management. It manages construction progress
                                                                                                                                                                                                                               The system checks data on record for each component and links
                                                                                                                                                          based on 3D BIM models. It supports data input by scanning           data to photographs.
                                                                                                                                                          QR codes* on construction components. Each component can
                                                                                                                                                          easily be managed end to end, from production through to
                                                                                                                                                          site delivery and installation. Using the volume, weight, and
                                                                                                                                                          other data from BIM models also lets us control work progress,
                                                                                                                                                          making construction management more efficient.




                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         OBAYASHI CORPORATE REPORT 2021
                                                                                                                                                              The system has been in practical use since February 2021
                                                                                                                                                          in the current construction of OY Project, an Obayashi Group
                                                                                                                                                          training facility in Yokohama that is Japan’s first fully-wooden
                                                                                                                                                          high-rise building, and since April 2021 in the construction of
                                                                                                                                                          the new ES CON FIELD HOKKAIDO, the future home stadium
                                                                                                                                                          of baseball team Hokkaido Nippon-Ham Fighters.
                                                                                                                                                          * “QR code” is a registered trademark of DENSO WAVE INCORPORATED.
                                                                                                                                                                                                                               Obayashi is using the system to manage the process of erecting
                                                                                                                                                                                                                               the fixed steel frame of ES CON FIELD HOKKAIDO’s great roof.

                                                             Slab                                                                                            Viaduct Project is proceeding near Fukui Station, between Kanazawa and Tsuruga on the Hokuriku Shinkansen.
                                         New method          replacement                                                                                         The railroad precast viaduct LRV construction method is an adaptation of the LRV construction method developed
         Nighttime (closure in effect): one lane closed
                                                                                                                                                             for reinforced concrete high-rise residential buildings. Used on railroad reinforced concrete rigid frame viaducts, it uses
                                                                                Six workers use mobile crane, aerial work platform, etc. to replace slabs.
                                                                                                                                                             precast beams and pillar-beam junctions. This cuts the construction time on the above-ground structure in half as
                                                                                                                                                             compared to conventional casting in place. Also, the only tasks that need to be performed on site are assembly of the
                                                                                                                                                             precast components and injection of the mortar. Onsite productivity rises because there is less time spent working on
                                                                                New method
                                                        Slab                                                                                                 rebar, formwork, and concrete and setting up the falsework for them. Quality is also greater than when pouring concrete
         Daytime: all lanes open                        replacement                                                                                          onsite, since pillar-beam junctions consisting of multiple components and having a dense rebar arrangement are made
                                                                                                                                                             in a factory.

                                                                                                                                                                                             Another example is dam concrete formwork, which until now has taken much time and labor. Conventionally, a crane
                                                                                                                                                                                        raised the formwork to the prescribed position for the pouring process, after which veteran workers on the site adjusted
                                                                                                                                                                                        it to the exact position and put the formwork in place at the next higher level. In contrast, the automatic dam formwork
                                                                                                                                                                                        sliding system we recently implemented has scaffolding that climbs automatically, a measuring system that confirms the
                                                                   Specific Measures Based on Business Strategy                                                                         formwork’s position and orientation data, and a driving system that moves the formwork back and forth.      The Construction Robotics Division aims to realize fully autonomous, eco-friendly construction using IoT, AI, xR,* robots, and                                                     its new place can be done automatically.           Obayashi has developed a logistics system that coordinates the operation of automated guided vehicles (AGV) with
          that of an elevator at a construction site. The result is autonomous transportation of materials across different floors of a
          building. The system looks up a transportation schedule that is input via the web. AGVs automatically transport materials
          from the reception floor to their destination. No operator is needed to run the elevator. Materials can be delivered to the                                                                                                                                                                                                                          ❺

          appropriate place at night or any time workers are not present.
